# KOTOR 2: super-deep character write-ups (Oct 19, 2026)

## Atris
*Corpus coverage: 195 own lines (every line, no length filter) · 106 lines naming them by name, alias or epithet · 28 pronoun references resolved from context · 16 lines addressed to them · 19 narration lines · 19 duplicate/variant lines merged (noted inline). Every «quote» is script-verified.*
### Background and history
Atris is the last Jedi historian, who stood at the Exile's trial. Was secretly drawn to the dark side by Sith holocrons [K2/Telos].
### Personality and motivations
Pride and judgment: «You sought adventure, you hungered for battle.» [K2/Polar_Plateau].
### Arc (scene by scene)
- **Polar Plateau:** judges the Exile.
- Leaks the Exile's return to bait the Sith [K2/Telos].
- **Telos (end):** fights the Exile and loses. Kreia: «she had destroyed herself» [K2/Trayus_Core].
### Relationships
- Exile.
- Handmaidens.
- Kreia.
### Abilities
- Lightning (vs the Handmaiden, first pass).
### Feats
- None major.
### Power-scaling statements
- «few have your strength» (about the Exile) [K2/Telos]
### Duels and fights
- vs Exile: loses.
### Verdict implications
- Mid Master.
### Open questions
- None.

### Quote bank A: Atris's own lines, by scene (page)

**[K2/Handmaiden]**
- «Betrayal.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «She has betrayed me... the last of your sisters has betrayed me. Betrayed us. She is your sister no more. She does not travel with the exile - instead, she has chosen to walk the path of the Jedi.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «It is the truth. And it is done. Do you doubt me?» [K2/Handmaiden]
- «It is a sign of his corruption. And perhaps hers as well. He will train her, and she will be flawed.» [K2/Handmaiden]

**[K2/Lost_Jedi]**
- «Is Revan your master now? Or is it the horror you wrought at Malachor that has caused you to see the truth at last?» [K2/Lost_Jedi]
- «We have not lost a Jedi this day. You felt it... she has lost herself. She is no Jedi - She walked Revan's path, but she was not strong enough.» [K2/Lost_Jedi] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «We are not the ones who taught him.» [K2/Lost_Jedi]
- «Yet they all stem from the same source. Her teachings violated the Jedi Code and lead all who listen to the dark side, as they did the exile.» [K2/Lost_Jedi]
- «Whatever that wound was, it was of the dark side. We should not have let her depart. She will simply join Revan again, or perhaps worse.» [K2/Lost_Jedi] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Malachor V should have been her grave. You saw it in her walk, and in the Force. It was as if she was already dead.» [K2/Lost_Jedi] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Master Atris: Telos.» [K2/Lost_Jedi] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K2/Polar_Plateau]**
- «I did not expect to see you again after the day of your sentencing. I thought you had taken the exile's path, wandering the galaxy. Yet you have returned - why?» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Your concern is noted... your friends have not been harmed. They have been detained, for their safety. I find it... unusual... that you are traveling with others again. I had thought you had forsaken the company of others after the war. Or is that why you are here?» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Your... slaves have been detained, for their safety. Though if that is how you treat them, I imagine they would prefer cells to your company. But you have not answered my question. Why are you here? The Order and you parted ways long ago, and the Order was the better for it.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Yet here you are. Perhaps you do not know yourself as well as you think. Regardless, your arrival here begs an explanation. Have you come to face the judgment of the Council, as you did so many years ago? Are you finally willing to admit that we were right to cast you out?» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «[[../Polar Plateau#Lost Jedi» [K2/Polar_Plateau] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «You presume much. The Force does not guide your movements, not any more. Its connection was lost when you fell. Perhaps you feel that the Force also led you to the Mandalorian Wars, as well. But in truth it only led to your exile.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Why? Because you turned your back on us... and the Order. You followed Revan to war against the Mandalorians. The very war that made Revan a Lord of the Sith... and ruined you.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «The Republic asked the Jedi Order for aid against the Mandalorians, that much is true. Yet that aid did not mean rushing into battle - giving in to aggression, your hate, your passions.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «So you said, so long ago. I didn't believe it, then, and I don't believe it now.» [K2/Polar_Plateau] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «So you said, so long ago. And look where it has led you.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Of course you were no Jedi. No Jedi behaved as you do. No Jedi would wish to behave as you do.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «You sought adventure, you hungered for battle. You could not wait to follow Revan to war.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «You, and all the Jedi who followed Revan, caused the Jedi Civil War - it was because the need to wage war burned within you.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Echoing the words means nothing unless you hear the source. I am not certain you have learned what the Jedi strove to teach you.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «The Jedi Order asked only for time to examine the Mandalorian threat. They urged caution, patience. And you defied them. So when you returned, you were brought before us. You were a Jedi no longer. And so you were exiled.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «There was much about that day that was difficult to forget - your words, your defiance - and when you stabbed your lightsaber into the center stone. I have kept it - so I would never forget.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Indeed. A lightsaber is the mark of a Jedi. When you turned your back on the Order, it was not yours anymore.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «I have always kept it, as a reminder of what can happen when your passions dictate your actions. I have kept it, so I would never forget your arrogance or your insult to the Order.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Become a Jedi again? That is a thing far out of reach.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «It is not yours. It is a symbol of something greater, which you no longer represent.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Then you misunderstand its meaning while it is in my possession - and what it now represents.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «So... you would fight me for it? You are not far removed from the monster who left the Order so long ago.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «But I am not unsympathetic to your feelings. Leaving the Order must have been difficult for you.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «I suspected as much. You cared nothing for the trappings of the Jedi... and you cared nothing for the Order, even on that final day you came before us.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Yet you gave the Council no other choice. You gave me no other choice.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Life is filled with choices. And when you are forced to make one, you have the Jedi teachings to guide you.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «So your choice was to meet the aggression of the Mandalorians with more aggression? That is not the Jedi way!» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «And you were ruled by hate and aggression! That is not the way of a Jedi!» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Every choice we make, whether we know it or not, sends echoes through the Force. It can awaken feelings, ignite passions, hate, anger, fear - where none existed before. By meeting aggression, by serving as an opponent against which the Mandalorians could test themselves, you fed their hate, their lust for war. And it sent a terrible echo through you. And because of it, you and those Jedi who met them on the battlefield lost their way... and you turned on us.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Of course I was. But the Jedi teachings require we examine how we may best help them - action without reflection is not our way.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «No, you lost yourself and you lost your way. It is the price of action without reflection... it is what we were taught as Jedi.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Then you would die a second death. Such is the price of action without reflection. It is what we were taught as Jedi.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Without you and the other fallen Jedi to support them, to feed their lust for war, Revan and Malak's crusade would have been over before it began.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «I know you betrayed the Jedi teachings. All that you had been taught, you threw at your feet and crushed them beneath your heel.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «You betrayed the Jedi teachings! All that you had been taught, you threw at your feet and crushed them beneath your heel.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «The Jedi teachings require we examine our actions... acting without reflection is not our way.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «There was no guarantee that marching to war would have saved the Outer Rim. In fact, quite the opposite.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «A physical victory perhaps, but the real victory lay in t-» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «There are victories other than physical ones. The real victory lay in th-» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Do not twist my words. A physical victory is not the only victory... or the only loss.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «You do not kno-» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «How dare you? The Mandalorian Wars should have been your grave and Malachor V is where you should have died!» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «What? What do you mean?» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «You are wrong, just as you were when you defied the Council.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «And that would have pleased you? So be it.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «You see shadows where there are none, and hate where there is none. You are blind, as always.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «It was only a battle, exile, nothing more. You had lost the true war long ago.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «I tire of... fighting with you. You lust for war, and you always will. And you have succeeded in distracting me from my questions.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «It will never be over. For either of us. But I agree that this discussion is pointless.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Your ship - ah, the Ebon Hawk? It is not your ship. Unless you are admitting to the destruction of the Peragus mining facility.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «The Ebon Hawk is here, safe. Its records and navicomputer are being dissected to determine what caused the destruction of the Peragus facility.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «We are having some trouble with the navicomputer... but I think with your cooperation, willing or otherwise, that will cease to be an obstacle.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «The destruction of Peragus gave me the right.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «If it is your ship, perhaps I should be questioning you as to what happened - and why you destroyed the facility and murdered all the miners stationed there.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «A facility of over one hundred and fifty personnel - all dead before you arrived. A childish story to mask your crime. And with the facility destroyed, you think there is no way to confirm your story. But I will pry the truth from you, I promise you that.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «At last your true nature shows its face. Serving with Revan truly corrupted you. I promise you this, murderer... I will see you tried and punished for what you have done. This is not the Jedi Council anymore - it is I who is judging you, and I will see justice done.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Again, you insist it is your ship. But it has had many owners, which I am sure you are aware. You have no claim over it - even if you did, the destruction you have already caused demands that you be tried and punished for what you have done.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «It was too much to hope that you may have come here to finally admit the Council was right.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «A lightsaber is the mark of a Jedi. When you turned your back on the Order, it was not yours anymore.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «I have always kept it, as a reminder of what can happen when your passions dictate your actions. I have kept it, so I would never forget your arrogance and your insult to the Order.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «You do not wish to fight for it? You surprise me. If you do not wish to fight, then answer my questions. What - happened - at - Peragus?» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Ah... an accident. Something beyond your control.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «So, at last you admit it. At least you have that much courage left.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «You will drown in lies before the day is done, it seems. Your admission is irrelevant - the guilt is thick on you.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Necessary? The destruction of Peragus was necessary?» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Of course you admit nothing. That would require facing and examining your actions.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «You have not changed. Acting instead of thinking. Putting yourself before the galaxy, before the Jedi. Do you know what you have done?» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «No, your crime is much more than that.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «[[../Polar Plateau#Trial» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Without the fuel from Peragus, Citadel Station cannot maintain its orbit. It will crash into the planet, and its destruction will echo across twenty other worlds.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Telos was a test, to see if the Republic could mount a restoration effort on the Outer Rim. When it fails, the Republic will not finance another. The other Rim worlds devastated by the Sith will remain graveyard worlds, devoid of life. And that is the magnitude of your crime.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «[[../Polar Plateau#Fuel» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «So you still hold to your flawed convictions. If you think to anger me, you are wrong.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «I have not known you since you left to serve Revan in the Mandalorian Wars. The only question that remains is how far you have fallen.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «How is it that you are not content to confine your ruin to yourself - you must spread it to others, wherever you go? Ruin yourself with your actions if you will, but when your actions bring harm to others, then you must answer for it.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Indeed? And how do you plan to seek your revenge on me? Or do you intend to take revenge on all the council as well?» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «So... it is as I suspected. You have already done enough. The dead Jedi, the murders - you have much to answer for, monster.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Jedi across the galaxy have been slain or have vanished. A darkness devours them, a new force shrouded by the power of the dark side. You are allied with them, as I suspected. You shall answer for your crimes, after I tear the knowledge of this new threat from you.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «You would say anything to avoid judgment... yet... I sense, somehow, that you speak truthfully. You are indeed ignorant of these events, or but a pawn.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Arrogant as always, even against the truths the galaxy throws at you. The Council was right to banish you - and you were wrong to return.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «We made no mistake. You deserved your fate.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «The Sith? What do you mean?» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «You speak truly... you have encountered the Sith. I can feel the scars on you.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Tell me - where did you encounter them?» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «And you encountered them on Peragus? But what would they want there? They can't have been looking for you.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Peragus... what would they want there? They can't have been looking for you.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «You? If they thought you a Jedi, the teachings of the Sith blind them, indeed.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «If you were the best target they could find, the teachings of the dark side blind the Sith, indeed.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «I am the last Jedi, not you. You betrayed our teachings, our beliefs... the very core of the Jedi Order.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «There are no more Jedi... except I. Like you, they turned from the Order, and now only I remain.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «If they sought a target, they would have hunted me, not you. But the blindness of the dark side is well known.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «If these Sith attacked you, they will soon realize their mistake. And if you escaped... they most likely let you go, to see if you would lead them here.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «[[../Polar Plateau#Ship» [K2/Polar_Plateau] *(+3 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «I said I was the last of the Jedi, exile, and I did not speak falsely. There are others who were once Jedi, but no longer. They will not take action against this threat.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Whatever force they can bring to bear, it will matter not - if they face a true Jedi, they shall fall.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «We shall see... for now, the perspective on your situation has changed.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «I have "your" ship. I will return it to you. You must leave here, before you place us in jeopardy.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «[[../Polar Plateau#Atris» [K2/Polar_Plateau] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «You shall find them north of here, in one of the old irrigation chambers. They have not been harmed... they are free to leave as well.» [K2/Polar_Plateau] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Take your ship, then - I don't care where you go, just leave this place, leave Telos.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «You offer your aid? After turning your back on me... on the council? The Jedi is not something you embrace out of fear. The commitment is stronger than that, something you never seemed to understand.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «[Success] Perhaps... but if you help me, it cannot be done from here. There are others in the galaxy who may help us against a Sith threat. If you can find them, gain their trust, perhaps our defenses shall be stronger for it. Take your ship, seek them out. If you find them, encourage them to gather on Dantooine - from there, we can call a council and see what can be done.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «Then I shall send you on your way... it is now time for you to depart.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «The exile reminded me of something... I had forgotten.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «We all have our heroes. And when we watch them fall, we die inside. She made a choice once... and I did not. The day we judged her, I stood in the chamber, and she was... she was so right. She was so certain of it, I doubted myself. But not now. She will never make me doubt myself again.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «The Jedi have no such attachments. As always, he will do as he wills, and the galaxy... and the feelings of others... can burn for all he cares. The day we judged him, I stood in the chamber, and he was... he was so right. He was so certain of it, I doubted myself. He chose Revan over the Jedi, over the Council... over...» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- «But now... now I am tired. I must meditate.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]

**[K2/Secret_Academy]**
- «She has left with the exile.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Her reasons are unknown to me. But I fear she may no longer be trusted. We will save her if we can, but we must let her discover the exile's nature for herself. Some evils must be confronted, and isolation from it would have been no defense.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Has there been any sign of the ship?» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «The freighter... it is important. And I thought the droid was as well. But I was wrong.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Then perhaps you will need to have faith that your sister will change her mind, remember her oath, and return to us... with the answers we seek.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Four?» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «Old woman?» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «I... do not recall seeing her.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «I am... tired. I... sometimes feel as if things are collapsing... all around us. It is just at the edge of perceptions, waiting. I fear... I fear things will collapse before our enemies reveal themselves.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- «I will meditate upon this. Perhaps it will clear my mind.» [K2/Secret_Academy]

**[K2/Telos]**
- «Who is there?» [K2/Telos]
- «I am Atris, Jedi Master... the last historian of the Jedi... the last of the Jedi.» [K2/Telos]
- «You are that which has attacked the Jedi... you are Sith.» [K2/Telos]
- «How did it happen?» [K2/Telos]
- «Why did she betray me?» [K2/Telos] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Where is the exile? I had thought...» [K2/Telos]
- «Where have you been? You have been absent so long. I feared for your safety. Were you with the exile all this time?» [K2/Telos]
- «Commanded? Did I command you to consort with him? To follow his teachings? To betray your oath?» [K2/Telos]
- «Of course you do not. But you will learn.» [K2/Telos]
- «Enough.» [K2/Telos]
- «Did you have feelings for him? Did you touch him, did you look upon him with love? There is no love in that one. He is a shell, devoid of emotion. All that he was died at Malachor, and he dies there still, as he should.» [K2/Telos]
- «So... one exile has arrived to save another.» [K2/Telos]
- «Do you care so much for her that you have come all this way? Perhaps you have feelings after all.» [K2/Telos]
- «It is no crime to kill the Sith or any that threaten the Republic... as you proved in the Mandalorian Wars. This is now my battle, and you are now my enemy.» [K2/Telos]
- «This is a weapon of a Jedi. You shall not have it.» [K2/Telos]
- «She swore an oath to me. Her life is mine, not yours.» [K2/Telos]
- «Yet you have come all this way for her - or perhaps it is just revenge that has driven you this far.» [K2/Telos]
- «Kreia? Oh... that is not her name. She is gone, and is now beyond your reach, exile. Where she has gone, only I shall follow... after I have dealt with you.» [K2/Telos]
- «Such a noble offer.» [K2/Telos]
- «Your execution has been too long delayed, exile.» [K2/Telos]
- «She said you would come here, to this place. If you think you can defeat me here, you are wrong. All this collected knowledge, all these teachings of combat and the Force - they are mine to command. And if I must use it to end you, I will. Surrender... you need not die.» [K2/Telos]
- «Atris... that is not who I am, not any longer. She has not existed for some time, I think. There was always something else within me - it just took time for its voice to be heard.» [K2/Telos]
- «The old woman you traveled with, finally made me... listen to myself, to the galaxy. She said that you would come here, and that you would face me in battle.» [K2/Telos]
- «She said you were the last obstacle to my enlightenment. If I wished to truly face the Sith, to see their heart, then that meant facing you, this last time.» [K2/Telos]
- «I do not know - yet. I suspect she went in search of you. But just as when she first came here, her path is difficult to see. She has set many things in motion, it is she that ordered the extermination of all Jedi, so long ago. She will answer for her crimes, in time. She is Sith... just as you are, just as all who followed Revan were.» [K2/Telos]
- «All the knowledge of the Sith, gathered from across the galaxy. Brought here by my servants, so that I might uncover their secrets - and use them to track them down. But now they have been drawn from the shadows of the Outer Rim - and the only final matter to attend to is finishing you.» [K2/Telos]
- «I do not know... yet. But it does not matter. They have come here to face the Republic in battle, and they will be destroyed. I will not deceive you... I knew what could happen there, but it had to be done to make the Sith reveal themselves. But I did not know the extent of their power - and what that meant for the Jedi. I will not underestimate them again.» [K2/Telos]
- «Yes, the Sith are here at last - you have brought them to this place, as I had foreseen. It has all been part of my plans for you. And when I defeat you and the forces you have brought to Telos, I shall take the battle to the heart of the Sith, and wipe them out – forever.» [K2/Telos]
- «These Sith are cowards, striking from the shadows to kill Jedi. I needed a target to draw them out - but I could not risk my own life, all that remained of the Jedi. So I arranged for you to return to the Republic, leaked information of your past, and then waited for the Sith to come. And they did. But you came to Telos, against my predictions. Now they are here, I can finally face this enemy and defeat them.» [K2/Telos]
- «Surrender? To you? Never. Let us end this.» [K2/Telos]
- «I fear nothing - least of all you. Let us end this.» [K2/Telos]
- «Very well. Let us end this.» [K2/Telos]
- «Kill me. End this.» [K2/Telos]
- «I did not expect mercy from you... here, at the end. After all that has happened between us.» [K2/Telos]
- «If you will not kill me, then what will you do?» [K2/Telos]
- «Of course... there is still something you need from me.» [K2/Telos]
- «This knowledge of the Sith... and the Jedi, is what I am. It is my attempt to hold on to the past... to try and protect the future.» [K2/Telos]
- «I sought to preserve the knowledge of the Jedi, and to do that, I needed to know the Sith, in order to stop them.» [K2/Telos]
- «Once, I was a historian, the chronicler of the Jedi. And when both wars passed me by, I was determined that I would not forsake battle again. In some part of me... I knew I had made choices, compromises, but always for the sake of the Republic, of the galaxy. To do what you had done... at times, did not seem so wrong.» [K2/Telos]
- «You are right. The Jedi were all that seemed left to me, and yet I had become so far removed from them that I betrayed them.» [K2/Telos]
- «To fight such a threat... sometimes, one's choices seem narrower than they are, until it seems there is no solid foundation on which to stand. I feel that I understand what drove you to battle. to fight the Mandalorians. It was something you could not turn away from.» [K2/Telos]
- «Where she walks is hidden from my eyes... but not from those of her kind. It is possible I may draw one last piece of knowledge from these holocrons.» [K2/Telos]
- «You always knew where they were striking from. You always knew. These Sith are spawned of you, spawned by the Mandalorian Wars... all those deaths, all those Jedi. Their power is to feed on life, until nothing is left except a hollow galaxy, echoing with the screams of the Jedi lost to us.» [K2/Telos]
- «Yes. I had thought she was awaiting me at that place, but I see now that she lied. It was not meant for me... but for you. She has gone there. She is waiting for you to travel to Malachor V, to finish what you started.» [K2/Telos]
- «Yes, you are an echo in the Force, a hollow space where it has been wounded. It takes a great act of destruction to create such emptiness, but it can be done. It creates places where the Force is difficult to hear, and difficult to find one's way. And you carry it with you, always.» [K2/Telos]
- «Now she seeks to create another echo, a wound in the Force, greater than the one before - greater than the one you caused. It will deafen all touched by the Force, until no life is left. You were strong enough to withstand it once - but few have your strength in such matters, especially if they are unprepared.» [K2/Telos]
- «I do not know. But she needs you there. If you choose not to follow, she will murder herself at the heart of Malachor, and you will die along with her.» [K2/Telos]
- «She seeks the death of all Jedi, all Sith... and the death of the Force. It is madness, it is impossible - but she believes you are the key.» [K2/Telos]
- «There are places in the galaxy dead to the Force, where nothing lives - where the echoes travel forever and do not reach their destination. And these places may be created, even from the simplest of events, the slightest of motions. One person, at the right place, at the right time, can change the face of the galaxy - or end it.» [K2/Telos]
- «Because the Force had been stripped from her... as it had been torn from you. At times, I wonder what we would be if the Force was taken from us - if we would truly be Jedi or Sith... or simply human.» [K2/Telos]
- «Because she proclaims to no longer follow the Sith. That she is something else, something that seeks "balance"... through destruction. She was a teacher once, and every student that she trained... has been a failure, and brought death to the galaxy.» [K2/Telos]
- «You are important to her, somehow. But I...» [K2/Telos]
- «...but I do not know for certain.» [K2/Telos]
- «[Success] She is willing to sacrifice herself at the heart of that graveyard world for you... a choice others have made in the past. A choice I wished to make. It is because I care for you. And I suspect that you alone hold that place in her heart, where nothing else lives. And that is why you are the only one who can stop the destruction to come.» [K2/Telos]
- «[Failure] I do not know for certain. If there is a reason, you must discover it for yourself.» [K2/Telos]
- «She is one of the Lords of the Sith, one of those who murdered the Jedi, and she holds the death of the galaxy in her hands.» [K2/Telos]
- «Because she has gone to Malachor. She is waiting for you there. But you will not survive Telos. Nothing will. It is because the greatest of her apprentices comes to Telos. And he will destroy everything, just as he destroyed Katarr and all the Jedi gathered there.» [K2/Telos]
- «And what will you do with me now? Abandon me here on this dead world - or end my life, as I wished to end yours?» [K2/Telos]
- «It does not matter. Not now - it is what I did with such pain that caused the wound. But I thank you.» [K2/Telos]
- «I tied my life, my decisions to the Jedi. Perhaps only in separating myself from the Jedi can I become myself again, learn who I am. Perhaps exile is what I deserve... even though it is many years too late, and you have already returned.» [K2/Telos]
- «Very well. It is more than I deserve... perhaps they shall grant me exile.» [K2/Telos]
- «I shall remain here, then. If Telos dies, our fates shall be tied.» [K2/Telos]
- «Leave now, while you can. Save Telos - save the galaxy. Save yourself.» [K2/Telos]
- «No... do not do this. If you leave me to them, in this state... I...» [K2/Telos]
- «No... please, I beg you... bring me before the council... have me face exile... But please... do not leave me...» [K2/Telos]
- «One thing I do not understand. The teachings at Malachor... why do they exist? They cause only echoes, wounds in the Force.» [K2/Telos]
- «A beacon? For who?» [K2/Telos]

### Quote bank B: what others say about Atris (by name, alias or epithet), by scene

**[K2/Disciple]**
- Disciple: «It is difficult to explain the difference between you and Master Vrook, but I think it is because he was knowledgeable, but not a leader, not a mentor. You were different - we could all feel it. And I knew that if I were to have a Master, I would want it to be you. And then you went to war. Many Jedi went to war, and the Jedi Masters proclaimed that you were Jedi no longer. Atris, the mistress of the archives, was first among them. I knew at that moment, that if you would no longer be a Jedi, then you must be correct. I realized I did not want to be a Jedi - instead, I wished to follow your path. And in any event, there was no one to train me, even if I wished it. They all went to war, as I grew past the age of acceptance.» [K2/Disciple]

**[K2/Goto's_Yacht]**
- Zez-Kai Ell: «Atris? But I had thought she had gone to Katarr, with the others.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]

**[K2/Handmaiden]**
- Handmaiden: «Those are Atris' records you have stolen.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «It is just me. And I am doing this because Atris believes you will need help.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «Yes, your features, your stance - Atris spoke of such Jedi who followed Revan, how their bodies came to mirror the dark side within them.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «No, it is what I believe. Atris did not believe such things.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «Atris says that you fell to the dark side in the Mandalorian Wars when you gave in to your lust for battle. Once you tasted war, you could not give it up.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Handmaiden: «Atris says when the dark lord Revan returned to the Republic, you did not march with them because you had fallen so far you could no longer feel the Force.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Handmaiden: «So it was a matter of choice, then. If Atris has erred in her evaluation of your motivations, it might be best to inform her.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Handmaiden: «Without having seen you and Atris fight, I cannot say. Battle is a pure form of expression. It is heart and discipline, reduced to movement and motion.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Handmaiden: «I believe that your leaving the Jedi Order may have hurt her more than she will ever admit. It is a difficult thing to speak of, to see Atris unable to confront such strong emotion within herself. You... you did not care for her, did you? Atris is beautiful. And wise.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «I see. I have asked you many questions - I did not mean to. They are questions that have gathered over years, and I did not wish to ask Atris.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «He was slain by Revan in the Jedi Civil War when Revan sought to destabilize the Echani worlds. Revan succeeded. The fact that our father chose battle is not shameful, but that is not the reason he went to war. He went to war to be with the one he loved, but not the one he had pledged himself to. He was disloyal. I am the mark of that disloyalty. It is said that such things run in the blood, and I have fought long to prove that this is not so. That is why I am different from my sisters. Yet I am pledged to them and Atris, and I would die before betraying them. I tell you this in trust, and ask that you not speak of it to others. I only wish you to know.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «Because when my father returned from the Mandalorian Wars, he walked as you do now. There was something wounded inside him. He did not speak of what had happened there. And with us, he was silent. Changed. When I look upon you, I see in you an answer to a question I have searched for all my life. And that is why I tell you this now. I do not believe you to be the monster Atris made you out to be. I believe your choice was my father's choice, and it was just as difficult.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «We attend Atris. It is the duty of all of us, from the first of my sisters to me, the last of the Handmaidens.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «Atris has already preserved it. What I wonder is if the Jedi teachings should not simply be stored, but brought to others. There is much in their approach to battle that is worthy to be shared.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Handmaiden: «Before entering Atris' service, yes, I carried a name, as all the children of the Echani do.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «It is not important. My title and rank is of consequence, not my name. I take value in Atris' service, not in myself.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «That is untrue. You and the Iridonian trust each other... or at least, the Iridonian trusts you. We heard much of the Iridonian when we served Atris. Atris believed that the Iridonian held the knowledge to restoring Telos.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «Training is something reserved for certain caste members of the Echani... but I do not see the harm in instructing you in some basic principles. I do not understand how you and Atris fight, but I will instruct you on how Echani children are raised on warfare.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «Atris was correct about you. You know war, its motions and currents.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «Combat among the Echani is... a personal thing. Repeated duels are not what they are in other cultures, and I would rather that this not become more than it is. And I have taken an oath to Atris against studying from a Jedi, or anything of the Jedi teachings.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «[Influence: Success] My father broke his oaths. He shamed us all. I do not wish to follow his path... I swore not to follow his path. If I were to follow a Jedi against Atris' wishes, then I would be betraying her. For you.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «This is a difficult thing for me to say, but I ask that you be silent as I tell you this. It is my desire to learn from you what you can teach me of battle. I have already learned much in our duels, but with every battle, I wish to know more of you. Your stance, your movements, I can sense shades of meaning, and an echo of something I have yet to experience. Atris said that you were the only Jedi to have survived the Mandalorian Wars. That you had stared into the heart of war, and only turned away because you were forced to. I do not believe her. I believe that you made a choice... as my father did. And that is important to me, more than you know. And you are important to me, more than you know. I will accept whatever you wish to teach me, though it breaks my oath to Atris.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Kreia: «You are spending time with the servant of Atris.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Kreia: «Revan welcomed her. And she was... said to be... a skilled warrior. Beautiful. And strong in the Force. The Force flows strongly in the blood of those born from Force Sensitives. I doubt that Arren was any different. If the servant of Atris is of her blood, then the potential lies within her. If you train her, if you teach her the ways of the Jedi, you will be asking her to break her oath to Atris. It would be best not to train her, and let the bloodline die with Telos.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Kreia: «But we were speaking of the servant of Atris - I would caution you to be careful of your interaction with her. She is not as... tempered as you.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Kreia: «Before you continue questioning me, I hope your thoughts in the matter concerning this servant of Atris are clear.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «[Influence: Failure] Even if I believed you, to even discuss this is a betrayal of my oath to Atris. We have spoken long enough - now I wish to be left alone.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «As I followed Atris, perhaps what I wished to follow was the call of my bloodline, of my mother. I have felt incomplete, hollow, since her loss at Malachor V... and with your help, perhaps this wound might be healed.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «I did not ask you to train me in the ways of the Jedi. I have seen that path - in Atris, and that is not the path I wish.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «I wish to follow you, not Atris.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «There is the betrayal of Atris, and there is the betrayal of my mother and father. After seeing you in battle, I know you more than I have ever known Atris. And I am not convinced that serving her is the greater good.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Kreia: «Something troubles you, servant of Atris.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «If you have come to ask me questions, try to find out more of Atris or the academy, then I have nothing to say to you. I see where your loyalty lies. And if you stand with her, then you cannot stand with me. She is of the Sith. She has faced battle, and been found wanting.» [K2/Handmaiden]

**[K2/Influence]**
- Handmaiden: «That was generous of you. I do not know if Atris would have been so kind - she has taught that you do not help others by giving them what they can achieve themselves.» [K2/Influence]
- Handmaiden: «Atris may seem harsh, but you dishonor her with such comments.» [K2/Influence]
- Handmaiden: «Perhaps the stories Atris told of you were untrue.» [K2/Influence]
- Kreia: «I am not speaking to you, slave of Atris. Be silent!» [K2/Influence]

**[K2/Khoonda_Plains]**
- Handmaiden: «Atris said that the Enclave on Dantooine was one of the great storehouses of Jedi knowledge - many Jedi came here to study the ancient holocrons and relics.» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]

**[K2/Kreia]**
- Handmaiden: «...if I was faster, I would no longer be the last of my sisters... but does Atris love him? Jedi do not love. Does Atris hate him? Jedi do not hate. This journey is harder than any she has sent me on.» [K2/Kreia]
- Kreia: «Do you think to turn her from Atris' will? If so, I hope your arrogance will prove true in time. But I will abide her presence. She may have her uses.» [K2/Kreia]
- Kreia: «Because Atris is a threat. And as much as she would try to use us against you, so may we use her servants against her. Do not see every enemy as an enemy - see them instead as an ally, whether they realize it or not. This situation may yet work to our advantage.» [K2/Kreia]
- Kreia: «[Influence: Success] Atris herself is not as familiar to me as perhaps she should be. Yet I feel I know her, yes.» [K2/Kreia]
- Kreia: «[Influence: Failure] I know her as much as I know any Jedi... if you have other questions, you may ask those, but on Atris, I can provide you no answers that you cannot find on your own.» [K2/Kreia]
- Kreia: «Because Atris' path is one I walked long ago, and it is a chapter of my life that has been read and closed. She has taken the first steps, I think - we shall see. Surely you felt the righteous anger, the spoken judgments, the lack of forgiveness.» [K2/Kreia]
- Kreia: «I was a historian once, gathering the relics of the Jedi, learning the ancient mysteries. Always, there were more questions. One quickly learns that the Jedi code does not give all the answers. If you are to truly understand, then you will need the contrast, not adherence to a single idea. That is why Atris and the others blamed me, sentenced me. They believed me responsible for Revan's fall.» [K2/Kreia]
- Kreia: «Like the servant of Atris, this one... has other masters. Though blind, she has ties to darkness.» [K2/Kreia]

**[K2/Lost_Jedi]**
- Master Vash: «We take responsibility, Atris, not cast blame.» [K2/Lost_Jedi]
- Master Zez-Kai Ell: «What would you have be done with her, Atris? Be mindful of your feelings! This is not Revan who stood before you. This one walks a different path.» [K2/Lost_Jedi] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K2/Nar_Shaddaa]**
- Kreia: «If you are so certain of your path then do what you will, servant of Atris.» [K2/Nar_Shaddaa]

**[K2/Polar_Plateau]**
- Kreia: «It is a place hidden from the galaxy, like the academy on Dantooine. But this place... oh, Atris... you have been clever.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atton: «Atris?» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Kreia: «You will not. If you do, then my silence will be broken. And then, Atton, you will be broken. You fear the Jedi and rightly so. If Atris learns of your... choices, you will never leave this place. But whatever fear you hold of the Jedi, know that if you disobey me, that my punishment will make you beg for the death that has long hounded you. Wipe the fear from your mind. You will not find blind obedience a difficult Master... you chose it once. You will learn to embrace it again.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]

**[K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]**
- Kreia: «Take me to Atris. She will have the strength to do what the Council cannot.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Atton: «She would, if she thought she was bringing Atris a prisoner. Especially a Sith Lord.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Atton: «She'll take her to Telos, and Atris will do what she'll do to anyone she thinks is a Sith.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Atton: «They'll take her to Telos, and Atris will do what she'll do with anyone she thinks is a Sith.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]

**[K2/Royal_Palace]**
- Master Kavar: «Atris? I had thought Atris had gone to Katarr. Too many Jedi have scattered - the council needs to gather, we cannot remain concealed any longer.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Master Kavar: «Atris? But I thought she died with the rest on Katarr. You've met other Jedi - I can tell. But you won't find me an easy opponent.» [K2/Royal_Palace]

**[K2/Secret_Academy]**
- Handmaiden Sister: «They were caged for their safety until we could determine your intent, exile. Atris cautioned us against your tactics, fearing that your allies would create a distraction. Your companions gave us little trouble, however. The male could have presented some challenge if he had resisted, but he chose not to.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «Your ship is stored in the hangar. Atris has given you permission to leave this place - and not to return.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «Your ship is stored in the hangar. Atris has given you permission to leave this place - and permission to return, if you remain in her service.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «We serve the Jedi, we do not question them. Yet... Atris has told us that the work here at Telos may pay for similar efforts in many worlds along the Rim that were destroyed when the Jedi turned on each other. She has said the Jedi Order needs such a foundation if it is to rebuild. She faults the teaching of many of the Jedi Masters as the spark of the Jedi Civil War.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «Atris has said that if Revan and Malak had been properly instructed in the ways of the Jedi, they never would have fallen... and nor would you, exile.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «Atris has said the training of the Jedi was flawed... and the Jedi Civil War was proof of this.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «That... is unknown to me. I have never observed Atris to teach Jedi, nor would I wish to.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «Atris has chosen instead to focus her efforts on galactic recovery. Once the galaxy breathes again, disciples will come.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Kreia: «So... Atris resides within the frozen heart of this world.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «Shortly before the destruction of Dantooine by the forces of the traitor Jedi Malak, Atris had many Jedi artifacts and knowledge transported here secretly.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «The Jedi Council sensed it, Master Vrook, Master Vandar... but it was too late to do much except make arrangements for evacuation. It was a dark day for the Order. Many on Dantooine did not survive... Jedi or not. Atris would not speak for many days after the attack, and we feared our mistress was lost to us. In time, she regained her voice and her strength. She brought the artifacts and the teachings of the Jedi here and has kept them safe.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «Viewing the relics is not allowed without Atris' permission.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «Atris speaks of you, but "Jedi" is not the term she uses. She says you followed Revan to war, the same Revan who took the Mandalorian flame and brought it to the Jedi.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «Atris speaks what she sees, and she sees much truth in things... and others.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «No, perhaps not... if you are truly a warrior. And not some murderer without discipline. But I do not believe you are weak or a murderer. You show reflection on your actions. Perhaps I misjudged you - perhaps you have changed from the exile Atris described to me. I hope you may show her the same side you have shown me.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «[Success] There is truth in what you say. Yet many such artifacts are sealed away in Atris' chambers. But if you wish to see a few of them, speak to the last of the Handmaidens. She has seen some of these objects, and has an... interest in such things.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «Atris is the only Jedi here, this is true. But the mistress will bring others in time.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «You are no Jedi, Atris has made that clear. When you went to war, you gave up the mantle of the Jedi and became something else.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «We have taken an oath to never follow the teachings of the Jedi, only to watch those of the Order and carry out Atris' will should it be necessary. Atris believes the new Jedi Order that shall arise here will need to be guarded and watched by those not touched by the Force. She thinks it will give balance.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «I would have struck you down as you entered this place if Atris had not stayed my hand. Though you are not a Jedi, you have been sickened in Revan's service. If for a moment I felt you could bring harm to Atris or this place, then I would end you.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «Atris is the one we serve, and she guides our actions.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «Atris is trusted. She has watched Jedi rise and fall, and knows the ways of both paths to see the signs when they begin. She is a worthy one to judge the Jedi when the Jedi refuse to judge their own.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «And therein lies her strength. We have known Atris many years, and she has shown much wisdom.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden: «You are the exile. The one Atris warned us about.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden: «Even if I possessed the senses that the Jedi were said to have, such teachings would be forbidden. Atris has set us upon the road we are to travel until the end of our days. We are to serve the Jedi by watching them, not learning their ways.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden: «[Failure] I cannot disobey Atris. My loyalty is to her, and to her word.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden: «I cannot disobey Atris. My loyalty is to her, and to her word.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Kreia: «To claim to know anything of Revan's choices or what lay in his heart when Malak fell is conceit, servant of Atris. And whether Revan had any choice in the matter at all is something else you should consider. The Force is a powerful thing to wield... or deny.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Kreia: «Ah, but at what point does the power the Force exerts submerge any attempt at choice, or free will? You have taken a complicated question, servant of Atris, and you have trivialized it with your answer... and lack of experience.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden: «The Jedi relics are kept by Atris, within the walls of her meditation chamber. Entry is forbidden.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden: «[Failure] I will not. Atris has made her orders on the matter clear, and she says you are no Jedi.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden: «[Failure] Atris has made her orders on the matter clear, and she says you are no Jedi.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden: «I have seen a few of them, yes. They were not always in Atris' meditation chamber.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden: «I... I have seen a few of them, yes. They were not always in Atris' meditation chamber.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden: «No, Atris has no bearing on this request. In fact, I think she would disapprove, quite strongly, if I were to ask.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «You are a strong opponent. Atris was correct about you - you know much of war and battle. Perhaps too much.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Kreia: «There is a Jedi here, perhaps. In that, you are correct. Yet there are no students, and this woman... this Atris... surrounds herself with those who cannot feel the Force. Curious.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Kreia: «Don't be a fool - Atris stole the ship and the droid.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «Why Atris would have sent her on such a mission is something I do not understand. In terms of combat, she is the last among us. In terms of stealth, she is willful and not prone to disguise herself. Her stance - it has too much passion about it.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «Perhaps that is why Atris sent her. Perhaps it is an exile of her own.» [K2/Secret_Academy]

**[K2/Telos]**
- Handmaiden Sister: «You have betrayed us. You have betrayed Atris.» [K2/Telos]
- Handmaiden: «Listen to me - Atris has been touched by the Sith, it is not too late for-» [K2/Telos]

**[K2/Trayus_Core]**
- Kreia: «I never destroyed Atris - she had destroyed herself. I merely stripped away the illusion, and brought her truth. Her teachings could not be allowed to continue. And like Malachor, she was part of your past, unresolved. She needed to be something you could confront - and defeat, one last time. It was part of your training. Part of what was needed to make you complete. And there must always be a Darth Traya. The galaxy needs its betrayers, especially in the times to come. She loved you, you know, as one loves a champion. You were all that she could not be.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- Kreia: «I used you to keep the Lords of the Sith from condemning the galaxy to death with their power unchecked. I used you to lure them to Telos, where they could be, at last, fought and killed. I used you to reveal Atris' corruption, so that her teachings could be ended before they began. I used you to gather the Jedi so they could be destroyed. And I used you to make those who wounded me reveal themselves, so they could be killed by the Republic.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- Kreia: «She cannot help but love you. It is a curious influence you have over her. If she leaves this place, she will leave battle behind her. She will take Atris' role as historian, and she will teach others of the Jedi who was not a Jedi... and who trained her to feel the Force. After that, I do not know. I do know that you must leave her behind. Where you are destined, you must not take anyone you love. It was Revan's choice as well.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- Kreia: «If she leaves this place, she will leave battle behind her... in no small part due to your influence. She will take Atris' role as historian, and teach others of the Jedi exile who gave up the Force... and became stronger for it.» [K2/Trayus_Core]

### Quote bank D: lines addressed to Atris (Heuristic: lines in second person spoken directly before or after one of this character's lines on the same page)

**[K2/Handmaiden]**
- Handmaiden Sister: «Mistress... forgive us, but are you certain? She would not forsake her oath so lightly...» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden Sister: «No, mistress. It is you who saved the Jedi upon Dantooine... who have collected their knowledge, and hid it here to protect it.» [K2/Handmaiden]

**[K2/Lost_Jedi]**
- Master Zez-Kai Ell: «Why did you defy us? The Jedi are guardians of the peace and have been for centuries. This call to war undermines all that we have worked for.» [K2/Lost_Jedi]
- Master Vash: «You are wrong. The dark side is not what I sensed in the exile. Surely the rest of you felt it as well. That emptiness we felt... she has changed.» [K2/Lost_Jedi] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K2/Polar_Plateau]**
- Handmaiden: «Are you all right, mistress?» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Handmaiden: «Forgive me, mistress... but I must ask. The exile... I have never seen another effect you so strongly.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Handmaiden: «Did you care for him once?» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Handmaiden: «Of course, mistress. I will tell the others you are not to be disturbed. And... please, do not exhaust yourself. We can attend to matters here.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]

**[K2/Secret_Academy]**
- Handmaiden Sister: «Mistress, we still do not know the significance of the ship or the droid. We downloaded all you asked, but we are still no closer to the answers you seek.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «Mistress, she was secured for much of the time the exile was here. At other times you were meditating, and we did not wish to disturb you.» [K2/Secret_Academy]

**[K2/Telos]**
- Kreia: «Those are titles, words you cling to as the darkness falls around you.» [K2/Telos]
- Kreia: «"Sith" is a title, yes, but like you, the title is not who I am. It is not what I believe. For you... it is different. Know that there was once a Darth Traya. And that she cast aside that role, was exiled, and found a new purpose. But there must always be a Darth Traya, one that holds the knowledge of betrayal. Who has been betrayed in their heart, and will betray in turn. You have bathed in the knowledge of the Sith. But there is not enough truth in such teachings... but it will be a step for you.» [K2/Telos]
- Kreia: «Search your heart. It was never battle that called to you. Never battle that caused you to fall. Malachor V has touched many things, and it casts its echoes still.» [K2/Telos]
- Kreia: «You betrayed yourself. Do not blame the exile. And unlike you and I, there is still a chance that one may be saved. The one that you cast out.» [K2/Telos]
- Handmaiden Sister: «We shall see... you were always the last of us. And now we shall show you why.» [K2/Telos]
- Handmaiden: «Mistress, as you commanded, I-» [K2/Telos]
