### Quote bank A: Azkul, Visquis and Loppak Slusk's own lines, by scene (page)

**[K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]**
- (Loppak Slusk) «You've got a lot of nerve coming here, Jedi, with the bounty we've got on your kind. What do you want?» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- (Loppak Slusk) «Good - it'll be that much easier to kill you. You're marked, human. Whether you're actually Jedi or not means nothing to me.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- (Loppak Slusk) «Yes, but I'm not a gambler. You're worth enough dead, and it's easier to ship Goto a corpse in a cargo container than a live prisoner.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- (Loppak Slusk) «[[../Bumani Exchange Corp.#Enough» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.] *(+3 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- (Loppak Slusk) «You - what? I gave very specific instructions regarding that hammerhead and any of his cronies. Who let this idiot in? Matu, bring me whoever's working the door. Nahata, tell Chodo if he sends anyone else, we'll come after him. And Benok... Dust this foolish Jedi.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- (Loppak Slusk) «Well, she'd better hope you succeed. I've grown weary of that pink tart's constant shrilling and plotting.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- (Loppak Slusk) «You - what? I don't have any ship of yours, human.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- (Loppak Slusk) «You and everyone else, human.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- (Loppak Slusk) «Enough of this. Kill her and anyone with her.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- (Loppak Slusk) «What? Whoever let you get back here better be dead, or they'll be wishing they were when my droids are through with you.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- (Loppak Slusk) «You've handled yourself well getting this far. But you should think things over carefully. I can be a very valuable ally. Just what is it you want? To join the Exchange? Money?» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- (Loppak Slusk) «Really, all this trouble just for just that?» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- (Loppak Slusk) «Ah, it seems we have some visitors. I was wondering when you'd show up, Luxa.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- (Loppak Slusk) «I assume this was all your doing?» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- (Loppak Slusk) «I suppose I should have known. You always were an ambitious one. Enough with that, though. On to the business at hand. I don't think it's possible that all of us will be leaving this room alive.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- (Loppak Slusk) «Only if that agreement involves Luxa's... termination.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- (Loppak Slusk) «No, wait! Don't kill me. You can be my right hand, the position's just become available.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- (Loppak Slusk) «I see. In that case, that'll be the last mistake you ever make.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- (Loppak Slusk) «Ha! You think you can kill the leaders of the Exchange that easily?» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- (Loppak Slusk) «Thank you for your assistance. I had suspected Luxa for some time, but I thought I'd let her show her hand.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- (Loppak Slusk) «Why would I want to do that? The Ithorians are ruining my profits.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- (Loppak Slusk) «Come now, surely you can do better than that? The Exchange isn't trifled with lightly.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- (Loppak Slusk) «I suppose you do make a bit of sense there. Fine, I will allow the Ithorians to do their business, but listen to me when I say this. The Exchange is not bullied. If you push, expect to be pushed back.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- (Loppak Slusk) «That's too bad. Hopefully Goto won't have a problem if there's a few holes in you.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- (Loppak Slusk) «I believe I'll choose to take my chances.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- (Loppak Slusk) «The Lorso woman expects me to leave because she asks? She's more foolish than she looks.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- (Loppak Slusk) «Negotiate? That isn't a word in my vocabulary. And anyways. You can't negotiate when you're dead.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]

**[K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]**
- «You are the Jedi I've heard reports of. And I am Azkul, leader of the mercenaries on Dantooine.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «Straight to the point. I like that. I will be equally direct. I am planning to take Khoonda, and you're going to help me.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «That is correct. And I want you to help me.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «You don't need to know the details. But I can tell you there are a lot of credits involved.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «According to my reports, I have four times as many soldiers as the militia, and I am committed to taking Khoonda. It is inevitable that I will succeed. If you wish to avoid my men eradicating the people of Dantooine, you will make it easier for me to take Khoonda. Of course, I will pay well for your services.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «There are many ways someone with your abilities can aid us. Before the action begins, I have two main tasks in mind for you: disable the gun turrets and the traps.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «Deal with the turrets however you see fit. Just keep them from pestering my men.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «When the militia learns of our attack, they will plant traps around the entrances to Khoonda. Disable them.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «Decide quickly. I have a slaughter to plan.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «Return to me when you when you are ready for us to begin our assault. I warn you not to keep me waiting.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «Are you ready? I won't wait long for you.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «Fine. What are you expecting, a reward? You'll get your payment when you see the job through to the end. The only thing I'm interested in hearing from you is that you are ready for us to begin our assault.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «You don't need to know. Suffice it to say, people much more powerful than you or I are paying a great deal of credits to have the job done.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «I'm not your friend, Jedi. If you're looking for stories, go read a datapad.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «You will be paid generously after you've done your part and we've taken Khoonda.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «[[../Defense of Khoonda#Handle» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «You'd better reconsider. I can't have a Jedi interfering with my plans. There is a considerable bounty on your kind that I will collect on unless you're working for me.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «Men, I trust you can handle this Jedi?» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «[[../Defense of Khoonda#Azkul» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «So, Jedi. I take it you are responsible for the surprises we've had during this battle. Stand aside. Our quarrel is with the distinguished Administrator. This isn't your problem.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «[Failure] Is that supposed to convince me, Jedi? I was trained by the Sith. My unit killed many Jedi. I'll give you one last chance. Leave the Administrator to her fate.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «She's inconvenient and doesn't know the chain of command. She will pay for that.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «We could kill you. But I think you'd slaughter at least four of my men. Recruitment fees are 250 credits per man. So 1000 credits for us to avoid the hassle of replacing those men. Do we have a deal?» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «You look capable for a Jedi. Fine. A full squad of eight, then. 2000 credits.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «[[../Defense of Khoonda#Administrator» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «You have no idea who you're talking to. I was going to spare your worthless life. I see I was in error.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «Then you've allied with these flaming settlers. The galaxy doesn't tolerate weakness.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «That isn't a possibility. This is going nowhere. You could've lived.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «This is going nowhere. You could've lived.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «That was my final offer, Jedi. Let's see the value of your boasts.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «I was trained at Malak's academy. Your tricks won't work on me. You're just another dead Jedi to me.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «I was wondering when you would betray me, Dopak. I'll send your final wages to your children, along with a detonator.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «Kill them all.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «You should have let him live. He would have fetched a fine price on Nar Shaddaa. Khoonda is ours, thanks in part to your assistance. Here, take your reward, Jedi.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «Your moral dilemma is none of my concern. Thanks again for your assistance.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «I don't see why I should give you more credits.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «You deserve more credits. You'll get fifty percent more.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «Thanks again for your assistance.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «[Success] Fine, greedy Jedi. You'll get fifty percent more. Take it and go!» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «[Failure] I'm already giving you more than you deserve. Take it and go!» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «[Failure] Pathetic, Jedi. Try your mind tricks on weak-minded fools like yourself. Take these credits and go!» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «Now if you'll excuse me. I have a date with the Administrator, and I'm dressed to kill.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «I was going to make it a painless death. But you've been much more difficult than you had to be. Now you're going to pay.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «You're free to go, Jedi. Just don't cause any trouble for us, or I will collect on that bounty.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- «Our business is concluded, Jedi.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]

**[K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]**
- (Visquis) «Ah, you have finally arrived. Please, come in and remove that cumbersome suit, the air here is quite suitable for your kind. Do not be taken aback by my hospitality - I assure you, no one will harm you as long as you are my guest and we keep things cordial between us.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- (Visquis) «Restrain yourself, Hanharr. There is no need for violence. I gather from your conversation that this is not the Jedi I invited here - it is difficult for me to tell, you humans all look so alike to me. Perhaps you would care to explain what I owe the honor of this visit? And where I might find the Jedi?» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- (Visquis) «Ah, what a prime example of human arrogance. What you know is substantially less than what I know. Otherwise, you would not have come. One, everything that goes on in the Jekk'Jekk Tarr is invisible to Goto. It is something I discovered by accident, and have tested many times since to insure accuracy. Second, I am not acting on Goto's orders - not anymore. Hanharr and I reached an agreement to deal with Goto altogether... and collect a tidy sum from Vogga.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- (Visquis) «He might, it is possible. But it is a risk I am willing to take. You see, Vogga is very tired of having his freighters hijacked by Goto - it is causing his word to have less... weight on Nal Hutta. There is a leak in his operations here on Nar Shaddaa. Rather than simply find the leak and eliminate it, his anger has compelled him to eliminate the source of the irritation altogether. Unfortunately, Goto is very careful about showing himself to others - Vogga's employee, Hanharr, discovered this when he attended the gathering on Goto's yacht. It is always holograms with Goto – always. But I have a suspicion that Goto will reveal himself to a Jedi. When that happens, that is when I intend to strike. Unfortunately, it is clear he will not reveal himself to a two-credit bounty hunter with the audacity to try and strike a deal with me. So please - tell me where the Jedi is. I am not in the mood for negotiation.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- (Visquis) «How predictable. Very well.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- (Visquis) «Take her away. It looks as if we will not be getting a Jedi this day.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- (Visquis) «Hanharr, please - calm yourself. The Jedi was on the way to meet us when sidetracked. We must be patient.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- (Visquis) «If she is a Jedi, perhaps she will come for her out of some misguided sense of protectorship. If not, then she will come to meet with me - and we may strike another arrangement.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- (Visquis) «Ah... finally, the genuine specimen. I had thought I might have to waste more time and resources baiting another trap. But the Jedi has come without an environmental suit - brave, indeed. We shall let the fumes of the Jekk'Jekk Tarr capture the Jedi for us.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- (Visquis) «I grow weary of these subtleties.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- (Visquis) «Let all the clientele know the Jedi... the real Jedi... has arrived. If necessary, remind them of the price for the exile's capture.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- (Visquis) «Hanharr, do not take me for a fool... they cannot kill the exile, only weaken her for capture.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- (Visquis) «But be silent - and let us watch the amusements on the monitors.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- (Visquis) «The exile has come far. Too far.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- (Visquis) «Trapped? No, Hanharr... as you know, many vents and shafts run beneath the Jekk'Jekk Tarr. You chased Mira through them once, if I recall.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- (Visquis) «Yes, it is a... private sanctuary. Stocked with other hunters as eager to capture Jedi as much as I - and you.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- (Visquis) «We will trap the Jedi there, then wait until the gases overcome her and make her fit for delivery.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- (Visquis) «We will trap the Jedi there, then wait until the gases overcome the him and make him fit for delivery.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- (Visquis) «Oh, indeed, Hanharr - against this foe, we stand together. But there is no more reason for us to remain here - let us go to the lower levels of the Jekk'Jekk Tarr, where the Jeedai cannot reach us.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]

**[K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]**
- (Visquis) «Yes, and impossible to enter if I do not wish it. So I chose my visitors carefully - and my victims. We may talk freely here, as we did in the Jekk'Jekk Tarr. It is my home, stocked with my soldiers, far from Goto's eyes.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- (Visquis) «They obey my orders, as do you. They are well-trained, know discipline, and despise Jedi. All that unpleasantness with the Republic bombing their world into a radioactive ball, you know, and the Jedi never moving to intercede. Such a tragedy... and from such tragedies, weapons and tools are forged.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- (Visquis) «Curious - you sound almost hopeful, Hanharr. Or is it concern I detect? Regardless, you presume too much. This clan has dedicated themselves to revenge against all Jedi - their will is focused on training to hunt - and kill them.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- (Visquis) «We shall see. But come, Hanharr, I have a gift for you. As a show of good faith. It will give us something to divert our time as we wait for the Jedi.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- (Visquis) «Be patient, Hanharr. The gas in the tunnels will take care of the Jedi for us. Besides, one who does not know the tunnels has no chance of finding the exit.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- (Visquis) «You think much of the Jeedai's chances. Even should the Jedi reach the exit, the door will not open - either back to the Jekk'Jekk Tarr, or here into my stronghold. It would have to be opened from the inside, and even if it was, then my guards would do what the gas could not. But enough - as I said, I have a gift for you.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- (Visquis) «Ah, Mira. I am pleased that my traps did not cause any permanent scarring.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- (Visquis) «It turns out the Jedi you decided to ambush has decided to come here anyway, no doubt to put an end to you. You know how base and vengeful humans can be. It would be amusing if the Jedi were to catch you. But I have other plans for you. The Jedi's attack has ruined certain... amusements I had planned. You must make those up to me, I think.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- (Visquis) «It turns out your Jedi friend has decided to come here anyway, in search of you. How touching. It will not be long before the Jedi lies unconscious in the tunnels, and I will send the Ubese to hunt it down.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- (Visquis) «But in the meantime, as boss of this sector, I must pronounce judgments on those who have crossed me - and reward those who serve me. Mira, I believe that you know this one - as he knows you. Hanharr, I have heard tales of how you have ripped humans in half. Indulge me. I have left her armed - I do not want her to die too quickly.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- (Visquis) «Hmmm. That was... unexpected. Still, I have the Jedi. And now I have no need to pay Hanharr for his services. I have other entertainments for you, I think. I am going to check on the Jedi - I will leave you here, with the payment I intended for Hanharr.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- (Visquis) «Oh, yes - your payment. After all, Vogga did pay me for my services. A portion of that payment is yours. Did you know that when the humans bombed Dantooine, few kath hounds were left? They fetch a rare price on the galactic market. Of course, the drawback is that one must keep them fed. So here is your payment, Hanharr - your credits, each with four legs, teeth, and very, very hungry. I shall send tenders in to collect your remains when they are finished with you.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- (Visquis) «Eh - the alarm? Who opened the door to the vent tunnels early? I ordered it sealed until I gave the command! How could the Jedi have opened it from the ins-?» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- (Visquis) «Hanharr.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- (Visquis) «Mira.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- (Visquis) «We must go to the arena - our bait has escaped. Deploy more of the clan to cover the base - we will need all of them. And tell them I no longer care if the Jedi lives or dies.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- (Visquis) «You have finally arrived - both much sooner, yet much later, than I'd hoped.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- (Visquis) «I will not be the one violating ettiquette - see you do not do the same.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- (Visquis) «Very well, let us dispense with the pleasantries. You are, after all, human.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- (Visquis) «Based on your actions alone, I take it you are not familiar with the organization I serve... or my responsibilities. I run the Refugee Sector - I decide what happens here, I control the flows and currents of this sector.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- (Visquis) «You have done much to aid the Exchange here on Nar Shaddaa. I wish to know why.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- (Visquis) «So you are the exile mentioned in the coreward database. Strange... I had not thought even an ex-Jedi would stoop to such depravities.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- (Visquis) «Join us? Now, why would I want such a threat at my feet? And why now? We have been hunting for you for such a long time, harming so many refugees to draw you out.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- (Visquis) «Then we do have something in common. How humorous. But I must ask - why now? We have been hunting for you for such a long time, harming so many refugees to draw you out.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- (Visquis) «You have caused a great deal of trouble for the Exchange here on Nar Shaddaa, and I wish to know why.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- (Visquis) «So you are the exile mentioned in the coreward database. It seems my squeezing of the Refugee Sector has yielded success, after all.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- (Visquis) «Oh, you have had it for quite some time. I must admit, once you were on Nar Shaddaa, you became difficult to track, but that is what bounty hunters are for.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- (Visquis) «We sought to put pressure on the Refugee Sector for a simple reason - whenever seeming-innocents are placed in danger, especially human fodder, there the Jedi are, lightsabers blazing.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- (Visquis) «Ah, of course. Information is such a high commodity here on Nar Shaddaa. And by virtue of coming to meet me, you have proven to have nothing I require.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- (Visquis) «That may be. But you were so slow to respond, I thought you might be nothing but a cantina tale.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- (Visquis) «It is curious, though - we have been applying pressure on the humans in this sector for some time, yet only now do you show yourself.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- (Visquis) «Ah, the Jedi have always been slow to act when danger threatens, more so now than in the past, and you are no different. Still - if you are only a recent arrival... that would lend some validity to the rumors of another Jedi being here on Nar Shaddaa.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- (Visquis) «Interesting. And well-spoken. If you are only a recent arrival...that would lend some validity to the rumors of another Jedi being here on Nar Shaddaa.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- (Visquis) «Fortunately for me, I require just the one.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- (Visquis) «Oh, you mistake me. I was not the one who put the original bounty on you - we all have our Masters, you know. My soon-to-be-deceased boss, Goto, is the one who placed the monumental sum of credits upon your head. Your price is so high that any bounty hunter who captures you would be able to buy their own planet. You must have angered Goto greatly for him to hunt you so. And that is why you are the perfect bait... and why I will bring you to Goto, then kill him.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- (Visquis) «The gas... it isn't... you... eh... I order you all - attack the Jedi!» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- (Visquis) «You all, attack the J... You all... you never truly worked for me.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- (Visquis) «Goto, I didn't...  I wasn't... Please, take the Jedi - I offer the human as a gift, freely, as I would my own li-» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]

**[K2/Landing_Rights]**
- (Visquis) «Welcome, {{s» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- (Visquis) «So... it seems the exile has chosen to come after all. Seal the entrance to the Jekk'Jekk Tarr. Unlock the sequencer to the private chamber, I will meet the human there.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- (Visquis) «You shall be compensated, Hanharr. The trap is not yet sprung - until then, I request your patience.» [K2/Landing_Rights]

**[K2/Suburban_(082_West)]**
- «You were looking for me, Grenn?» [K2/Suburban_(082_West)]
- «Sounds like you need someone to punch a hole for you.» [K2/Suburban_(082_West)]
- «All right then. You'll have one Sith-sized hole coming up. Let's go, boys.» [K2/Suburban_(082_West)]
- «Well, boys. The TSF's in over their heads and they need us to rescue their hides. Simple enough. We just need to get out there and face down a squad of Sith soldiers. Time to earn our pay.» [K2/Suburban_(082_West)]

### Quote bank B: what others say about Azkul, Visquis and Loppak Slusk (by name, alias or epithet), by scene

**[K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]**
- Vula: «Excuse me. Loppak Slusk's appointment has arrived. Please open the inner door. There you are. Have a good day!» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- Vula: «Loppak Slusk should be waiting within. Have a good day!» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- Luxa: «Slusk.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- Luxa: «Slusk's dead, but Goto's still my boss. I'm shipping you to Nar Shaddaa, "Jedi," dead or alive. If you don't want to defend yourself, that's your choice.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Atton: «The Exchange is one of the most powerful groups on the Smuggler's Moon. If this Goto wants you so badly, it'd probably be a good idea not to show up on his doorstep, wouldn't you say? Not to mention, I'm sure Loppak Slusk has friends on Nar Shaddaa that won't be happy to hear he's gone.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- Security Terminal: «[You can't access this terminal with Slusk standing there.]» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- Terminal: «[Success] Welcome, Loppak Slusk.<br><br>MAIN COMMAND CONSOLE» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- Luxa: «Slusk's dead, but Goto's still my boss. I'm shipping you to Nar Shaddaa, "Jedi".» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]

**[K2/Cantina]**
- Benok: «You haven't been on Citadel Station long, have you? Let me introduce myself. I'm Benok, the man in charge of Loppak Slusk's protection. You do know who Loppak Slusk is, don't you? The two gentlemen with me are Matu and Nahata, Slusk's finest men. Other than me, of course.» [K2/Cantina]
- Benok: «Now, Luxa, I meant no disrespect. You're probably Slusk's strongest woman.» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «It's your skin, Benok. Just warning you - Slusk could easily replace you.» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «Is that all? What a disappointment. But yes, what you were told is true, I can arrange meetings with Loppak Slusk, but you'd have to give me a good reason.» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «No? How disappointing. But don't worry, in addition to setting up a meeting with Slusk for you, I'll also pay you for services rendered.» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «Before you say anything - yes, I will arrange the meeting with Slusk that you desired. But first, I was wondering if you might listen to a proposition. A business proposition, given your obvious abilities. Let me ask you an honest question - this meeting with Slusk you want, it's because Lorso's fed up with him, right? I feel the same way about Slusk, and I think it's time someone solved the problem. I think that maybe our interests lie in the same direction.» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «Now I know you're not dull enough that you can't read through the lines. Slusk's no good for anybody's business - yours, Lorso's or mine. [[../Cantina#Goto» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «I handle vice - you know, spice, gambling, the good things in life - for the local Exchange boss, Loppak Slusk.» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «This Exchange bounty has nothing to do with you - but Slusk, my boss, won't listen to me. Still, you seem a very capable sort. Tell me, are you a capable sort?» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «So here it is - help me with Loppak Slusk, and I'll keep the Exchange off your back.» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «What, besides his being your typically slimy Quarren? Slusk works for Goto, out of Nar Shaddaa. Now this Goto; he's rigid, ruthlessly efficient, and all he sees are numbers.» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «Goto keeps the squid around because he maintains a steady flow of income... yet it's only half of what I know it could be. I should be Citadel's boss - but Goto doesn't allow breaks in the chain of command. I can't go over Loppak Slusk's head, so I've got to take care of him myself. And I want your help.» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «Look - until Slusk's out of the picture, he'll just keep sending men after you.» [K2/Cantina]
- Luxa: «When Slusk's out of the way, I'll clear up this bounty matter. Deal?» [K2/Cantina]

**[K2/Czerka_Offices]**
- Jana Lorso: «You proved that you can handle yourself quite well in dealing with the retrieval of the droid intelligence. The next matter of business I would ask of you is much more delicate. When we first set up here on Citadel Station, the Exchange was already quite well established. Loppak Slusk had more influence over business matters than the Telosian government. To ensure our success, we involved ourselves with the Exchange, and now that we have a handle on the situation, we wish to un-entangle ourselves from them. If you know anything about the Exchange, then you know that they would not take this well. Slusk is loathe to have his hands removed from any business on the station, so a more direct method will have to be taken. To put it plainly, I want you to kill Loppak Slusk.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- Jana Lorso: «I realize it won't be an easy task, as Slusk keeps himself well protected. You would be well compensated for it.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]
- Jana Lorso: «I have a feeling they won't just let you march into their offices. Luxa is Slusk's second. She spends little time in the Exchange offices, though, preferring the cantina in the Urban module. She might be able to set up an appointment.» [K2/Czerka_Offices]

**[K2/Dantooine]**
- Saedhe: «I'm not going to lie, he would be very interested. But Azkul is a low-life. We did it to protect the settlers of Dantooine. I'll pay 5000 credits for all three sensors. We're not a prosperous colony, that's really all we can afford. Please help us and make some money.» [K2/Dantooine] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Saedhe: «I'm going to alert the militia and let them know you intend to sell us out to Azkul. You won't get away with this!» [K2/Dantooine] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]**
- Mandalore: «Watch out for Azkul. I've known his kind before. He will attack you without hesitation if he believes you aren't working with him.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Zherron: «I don't know for certain. It could be a day, or even a week. Depends on how good Azkul is at organizing his men. I'm guessing not so good.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Zherron: «It's not that the settlers aren't good people. Many folk just don't have the fighting mentality. They'd rather just make people happy and get along. But there isn't any getting along with Azkul, he's out for blood and taking the place over. So we got to protect the Administrator. Khoonda can go on without me, but not without her.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Zherron: «Afraid not. We don't have much time. We got reports that Azkul is almost ready.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Dopak: «I didn't sign on for this cold-blooded slaughter, Azkul.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Dopak: «It was pretty brave of you to stand up to Azkul like that. Your resolve made me finally take action against him.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]

**[K2/Droid_Warehouse]**
- Mira: «We took out Visquis, but Goto has her... and that means no bounty for me.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Mira: «No one knows how to reach Goto except Visquis, and that squid-head died in the Jekk'Jekk Tarr.» [K2/Droid_Warehouse]

**[K2/Enclave_Courtyard]**
- Dopak: «A sentient by the name of Azkul. He was an elite trooper for Malak during the Jedi Civil War. He knows how to wage war.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]
- Nallek: «All the time. They tell us to be careful. Just scare them and make sure we get anything we want. Except Khoonda we got everything under control. There's been a couple of people we had to get rid of. But I wasn't on those trips. Azkul's got it all figured out.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]

**[K2/G0-T0]**
- G0-T0: «[[../Defense of Khoonda#Azkul» [K2/G0-T0] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- G0-T0: «I will concede Visquis was effective in using the Jekk'Jekk Tarr to cloak his movements - a clever organic deception, indeed.» [K2/G0-T0]

**[K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]**
- Devaronian: «Visquis? You must be a special visitor indeed... proceed past the green toxin rooms, and you will find Visquis in the humidity chambers.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- Twi'lek Domo: «Visquis? Ah, the Quarren. Unless you are here on his invitation, I could not tell you. It is said that he has many chambers beneath this place, but that would merely be rumor and supposition, not verifiable fact.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- Mira: «You've signed on with Vogga the Hutt? Visquis, you're dumber than I thought. There's no way Goto won't find out.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- Zez-Kai Ell: «I have watched you as you have traveled the Refugee Sector. I've seen what you have done... what I refused to do. Even exiled, you are more a Jedi than I. If anything, know that your actions have convinced me I can stand by and watch no longer while the Exchange closes its grip on this sector. I know a young woman went to meet with Visquis in your place. He will not negotiate with her - he will kill her. I intend to rescue her. I will return shortly... or not at all.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- Zez-Kai Ell: «If anything, you should know that your actions have convinced me I can stand by and watch no longer. Whatever harm the Exchange harbors against Jedi, I would not let another suffer for it. A friend has gone to meet Visquis in your stead, and I intend to rescue her. I will return shortly... or not at all.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- Twi'lek Attendant: «Yes... she came here. She did not know of all the traps that Visquis has in this chamber, and she was caught by surprise.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- Twi'lek Attendant: «Yes... Visquis is working with him. He is a monster, and if he guards Visquis, then you are in terrible danger.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]

**[K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]**
- Emergency Tunnel Control: «Based on the area schematics, there are only two entrances into Visquis' base. One is through the tunnels from the Jekk'Jekk Tarr. The other is a private emergency tunnel, a pre-programmed escape route to the Nar Shaddaa docks in the event the security door to the vent tunnels is opened.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- Hanharr: «No! Visquis dead... the Jeedai gone! Wh... And this tunnel is sealed - there is no way back into the Jekk'Jekk Tarr! That means Goto has the Jee-» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- Hanharr: «I thought I smelled a human, waiting like a coward at the end of this escape tunnel. Were you waiting for Visquis, human? If so, you are too late. But not too late to die. Eh? Why is the do-» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]

**[K2/Khoonda]**
- Terena Adare: «[Success] We need the skills of a Jedi more than ever. He was helping investigate Azkul and his mercenaries. But he had his own errand to run in the Enclave. Jedi business of some sort. He went into the sublevel, which isn't without its dangers. And he hasn't returned.» [K2/Khoonda]
- Terena Adare: «Mercenaries have been a problem for Dantooine since the end of the Mandalorian Wars. When the Jedi Enclave was here, they were firmly kept in check. But now... Relations are uneasy between us. Their leader, Azkul, was a Sith Trooper during the war, and I have no doubt he was very good at his job. Until they find work, tensions will grow. If you want to know more, I suggest you talk with Berun in the militia headquarters. He would have more details.» [K2/Khoonda]

**[K2/Landing_Rights]**
- Kreia: «It may be a trap - but traps work both ways. This Visquis - his kind are spread through the lower reaches of Nar Shaddaa, and he may have information. But the choice is yours. If you go, you will have to go alone.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- Mira: «I'm Mira. I'm the best bounty hunter in this system, and that's not me bragging, that's fact. I had you in my sights ever since you landed. I've been watching you run all over the Refugee Sector like a bantha, and for someone with a price on their head as high as yours, you sure don't know how to keep a low profile. Look - I know that squid-head Visquis sent you a message to meet him in the Jekk'Jekk Tarr. He works for Goto, and it's a trap. I'm betting he's going to lure you in there, start a fight, and then he's going to wrap you up and deliver you to Goto, dead, claiming you attacked him.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- Mira: «Just this - I know you're meeting with Visquis.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- Mira: «Well, whatever your reasons are, the fact you're meeting with Visquis is what I want to talk about.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- Hanharr: «Visquis is said to hold second Jedi. Visquis is keeping him safe, letting the bounty rise before giving him to Goto to get more honor.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- Hanharr: «I know you are going to meet Visquis. And you know that Visquis will try to capture you, sell you to Goto.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- Hanharr: «Everyone knows. Visquis sent a broadband com message to Jeedai's vessel, which was caught by all with the right receivers. And if I know, then now everyone knows.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- Hanharr: «You are strong - I can smell the blood on your hands, and hear the many dead that scream silently behind you. But Visquis make error in contacting Jeedai - he is trying to cheat all hunters by robbing us of prey and capturing you himself. But I have a plan... continue to the Jekk'Jekk Tarr, I will go ahead, and I will get close to Visquis. Then we will strike a new deal - for you, for me, for Jeedai.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- Mira: «You know Visquis is arranging a trap for you in the Jekk'Jekk Tarr. No surprise there. Thing is, he intends to cut the bounty hunters out of the loop and deliver you to Goto personally. Hm. Not smart from where I'm standing.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- Mira: «Well, for one thing, you're going to stay here, and I'm going to meet with Visquis instead.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- Mira: «Oh, you will. Just not right now. In fact, you're going to stay here until I meet with Visquis.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- Mira: «An ex-Jedi with a death wish doesn't concern me - unless credits are involved. That's why you're going to stay here until I meet with Visquis.» [K2/Landing_Rights]

**[K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]**
- Refugee: «Well, they work for the Exchange - for a Quarren named Visquis. He's looking to step up in the Exchange. The only language the Exchange respects is money, so Visquis is trying to increase his profits by using the refugees here in Nar Shaddaa as a cheap labor force. We're only good to him as slaves and merchandise. He wants to keep us in one place so he can control us. That's always been the way... well, except lately.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]

**[K2/Refugee_Quad]**
- Aaida: «'Deal with the Exchange?' You might as well wrestle with a planet. This branch of the Exchange is run by Saquesh, a Quarren with ties to Visquis himself.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]
- Aaida: «Visquis is the Exchange boss here on Nar Shaddaa, at least in this sector. You don't want to cross him - there's usually a battalion of hired guns lurking in the shadows behind him.» [K2/Refugee_Quad]

**[K2/Residential_082_East]**
- Exchange Thug: «Vula, a human is here to see Slusk. Everything checks out. Please open the door.» [K2/Residential_082_East]
- Exchange Thug: «Loppak Slusk is a busy man, he doesn't speak with just anyone.» [K2/Residential_082_East]

**[K2/Residential_082_West]**
- Chodo Habat: «I have tried to meet with the leader of the Exchange here, a Quarren called Loppak Slusk. He has refused my every invitation, and allows no one inside the Exchange suites. I fear he does not take us seriously. The Jedi, though, are renowned as warriors and diplomats both. Perhaps you might speak with Loppak, show him the value of a restored Telos.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «Loppak Slusk is difficult to meet with, is he not? I will continue to guide my thoughts towards your success.» [K2/Residential_082_West]
- Chodo Habat: «You have returned safely! Did your meeting with Loppak Slusk go well?» [K2/Residential_082_West]

### Quote bank D: lines addressed to Azkul, Visquis and Loppak Slusk (Heuristic: lines in second person spoken directly before or after one of this character's lines on the same page)

**[K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]**
- Gamorrean Boss: «Boss! Matu is here with Koobis, from the door. You want to see him now? Yes, I will send him.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- Luxa: «Not as soon as you might think.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]
- Luxa: «Well, that's all wrapped up. I've got to thank you for your help.» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]

**[K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]**
- Kreia: «Well maneuvered! With both the mercenaries and the militia counting on your aid, you can influence the outcome of this situation as you see fit.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Handmaiden: «Then I am glad, but I still disapprove of your actions. Using the tools of deception and manipulation will lead to you the dark side.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- HK-47: «Statement: I approve of your style, Master.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Master Vrook: «Sorry I was held up. But it looks like I've arrived in time to pull you out of your predicament.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Terena Adare: «Thank you so much for your aid. You can be sure that Dantooine will not forget how the Jedi protected us from this threat.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Terena Adare: «The Republic won't stand for what you've done here. You'll pay for your crimes.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Daraala: «I heard you were responsible for driving the settlers off this rock. Thanks. Now we can salvage all we like free of Khoonda's regulations and fees. I've still got some Jedi junk left to sell. You buying?» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]

**[K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]**
- Hanharr: «You!» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- Mira: «Good eyes, Hanharr. No wonder you're still number two on Nar Shaddaa.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- Mira: «Yeah, and maybe you'd like to explain why you've decided to backstab Goto and claim the Jedi for yourself.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- Mira: «Yeah, right. I'm not telling you where the Jedi is - it's my bounty, and that means she's under my protection.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Zez-Kai Ell: «If you have come to this moon for answers - or for revenge, then you will follow me. For if I fail, then you will be denied both.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- Kreia: «:: Listen to me - clear your thoughts. ::» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- Hanharr: «You think they can stop the Jeedai? She will carve through them like a blade.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Hanharr: «The Jeedai is a predator. You have baited a trap, but only you are caught.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- Hanharr: «So it is as I heard. You do have a base beneath the Jekk'Jekk Tarr to trap the Jeedai.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- Hanharr: «Then I will go with you to your base... and I will help you trap the Jeedai. After all... we are allies in this. You would not betray me as you betrayed the other hunters.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]

**[K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]**
- Hanharr: «This is your stronghold. It is well-hidden.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- Hanharr: «You trust this clan of Ubese to walk among you. Even through their environmental suits, they smell of hate, barely controlled.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- Hanharr: «You trust in their hate, not their skill. They cannot be controlled. The Jeedai will murder them.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]

**[K2/Landing_Rights]**
- Mira: «I was saving this dart for you, Hanharr, but right now, I need one less Jedi running around. Good thing I upped the dosage enough to take down a ronto.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- Hanharr: «I have brought the Jedi to you. Now where is Goto?» [K2/Landing_Rights]

**[K2/Suburban_(082_West)]**
- Lieutenant Grenn: «You look terrible, what happened to you? Some sort of plague going around?» [K2/Suburban_(082_West)]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «About time you showed up - the Sith are moving to keep us trapped in here. We need to push them back so we can get reinforcements deployed to the rest of the station.» [K2/Suburban_(082_West)]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «Damn! Zherron, I need you and your men to break through and lead the assault.» [K2/Suburban_(082_West)]
